## Clean Fuel Regulations {#clean-fuel-regulations .Title-of-Act}

[SOR]{.abbr title="Statutory Orders and Regulations"}/2022-140
:::

[CANADIAN ENVIRONMENTAL PROTECTION ACT, 1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)

Registration 2022-06-21

Clean Fuel Regulations

P.C. 2022-704 2022-06-20
::::

:::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::::: section
::: {.section .order}
Whereas, under subsection 332(1)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_a){#fn_a-ID0EICDA} of the [Canadian
Environmental Protection Act, 1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_b){#fn_b-ID0EFCDA}, the Minister of the
Environment published in the [Canada
Gazette](http://www.gazette.gc.ca/), Part I, on December 19, 2020, a
copy of the proposed [Clean Fuel
Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2022-140), substantially in the
annexed form, and persons were given an opportunity to file comments
with respect to the proposed Regulations or to file a notice of
objection requesting that a board of review be established and stating
the reasons for the objection;

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^a^](#fn_a-ID0EICDA){#fn_a}S.C.
  2004, c. 15, s. 31
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_b-ID0EFCDA){#fn_b}S.C.
  1999, c. 33
  :::

Whereas the Governor in Council is of the opinion that the proposed
Regulations could make a significant contribution to the prevention of,
or a reduction in, air pollution resulting, directly or indirectly, from
the combustion of liquid fossil fuels;

And whereas, under subsection 140(4) of that Act, before recommending
the proposed Regulations, the Minister of the Environment offered to
consult with the governments of the provinces and the members of the
National Advisory Committee who are representatives of Aboriginal
governments;

Therefore, Her Excellency the Governor General in Council, on the
recommendation of the Minister of the Environment, makes the annexed
[Clean Fuel Regulations](/eng/regulations/SOR-2022-140) under sections
140[[Footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#fn_c){#fn_c-ID0EHCAA} and 326 and
subsection 330(2) of the [Canadian Environmental Protection Act,
1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^b^](#fn_b){#fn_b-ID0EECAA} and subsection 5(1) of the
[Environmental Violations Administrative Monetary Penalties
Act](/eng/acts/E-12.5)[[Footnote
]{.wb-invisible}^d^](#fn_81000-2-3718_hq_27998){#fn_81000-2-3718_hq_27998-ID0EBCAA}.

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote ]{.wb-invisible}^c^](#fn_c-ID0EHCAA){#fn_c}S.C.
  2008, c. 31, s. 2
  :::

- ::: Footnote
  [[Return to footnote
  ]{.wb-invisible}^d^](#fn_81000-2-3718_hq_27998-ID0EBCAA){#fn_81000-2-3718_hq_27998}S.C.
  2009, c. 14, s. 126
  :::
:::

## [Interpretation]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1358715 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Definitions

- **[[1]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-1 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  following definitions apply in these Regulations.

  [[account holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[account holder]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to any
      account opened under section 28, means the primary supplier or
      registered creator for whom the Minister opened the
      account. ([titulaire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Act]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the [Canadian Environmental
      Protection Act, 1999](/eng/acts/C-15.31). ([Loi]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[authorized agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[authorized agent]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means, 

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in respect of a corporation, any officer of the
        corporation who is authorized to act on its behalf;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} in respect of an individual, that individual or
        any person authorized to act on behalf of that individual; and

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} in respect of any other entity, any person
        authorized to act on behalf of that entity. ([agent
        autorisé]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[baseline carbon intensity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[baseline carbon intensity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      weighted average carbon intensity of the gasoline or diesel used
      in Canada for the year 2016, as set out in subsection
      5(3). ([intensité en carbone de base]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[biogas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[biogas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a gaseous mixture that is
      recovered from the anaerobic decomposition of biomass and that
      consists primarily of methane and carbon dioxide and contains
      other constituents that prevent it from meeting the standard for
      injection into the nearest natural gas
      pipeline. ([biogaz]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[biomass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[biomass]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the biodegradable fraction
      of products, waste and residues of a biological origin ---
      including plant and animal substances --- originating from
      agriculture, forestry and other industries, such as fishing and
      aquaculture, as well as the fraction of waste, including
      industrial and municipal waste, of a biological
      origin. ([biomasse]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[carbon intensity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[carbon intensity]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, in relation to a fuel,
      energy source, or material input that is renewable natural gas,
      biogas, renewable propane or hydrogen, means the quantity in grams
      of CO~2~e per megajoule of energy contained in that fuel, energy
      source or material input that is released over the life cycle of
      that fuel, energy source or material input, including during the
      activities carried out during the stages of the life cycle, such
      as

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the extraction or production of the feedstock
        used to produce the fuel, energy source or material input;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the processing, refining or upgrading of the
        feedstock to produce the fuel, energy source or material input;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the transportation or distribution of the
        feedstock, of intermediary products or of the fuel, energy
        source or material input; and

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the combustion of the fuel. ([intensité en
        carbone]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[carbon-intensity contributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[carbon-intensity contributor]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      person who applies for the approval of a carbon intensity under
      subsection 80(1) for a set of activities carried out over the life
      cycle of a fuel in the liquid class or a low-carbon-intensity fuel
      with the intention to transfer the approved carbon intensity to a
      registered creator or foreign supplier or to another
      carbon-intensity contributor. ([contributeur à l'intensité en
      carbone]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[charging-network operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[charging-network operator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
      who operates a communication platform that collects data on the
      electricity supplied by a charging station and who is the owner of
      that data. ([exploitant d'un réseau de recharge]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[charging-site host]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[charging-site host]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who owns
      or leases a charging station and who has the legal right to have
      the charging station installed.  ([hôte d'une station de
      recharge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"}) 

  [[charging station]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[charging station]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a device that is
      used in Canada to charge the battery on board an electric vehicle
      by supplying electricity to the electric vehicle and that is
      capable of communicating with a server, whether through the
      Internet or using a cellular signal or connected vehicle
      communications, to report the quantity of electricity supplied and
      the time at which it is supplied. ([borne de
      recharge]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[CO~2~e]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[CO~2~e]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the quantity of carbon
      dioxide, expressed in grams or tonnes, that would be required to
      produce a warming effect equivalent to another greenhouse gas over
      a particular period of time, as set out in the Fuel LCA
      Model. ([CO~2~e]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[compliance-credit transfer system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[compliance-credit transfer system]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means
      the system administered by the Minister for the transfer of
      credits in accordance with sections 105 to 112.  ([mécanisme de
      cession des unités de conformité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[compliance period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[compliance period]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the period that begins on the day on which
        these Regulations are registered and ends on December 31, 2022;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the period that begins on January 1, 2023 and
        ends on June 30, 2023;

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the period that begins on July 1, 2023 and ends
        on December 31, 2023; or

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} after December 31, 2023, each calendar
        year. ([période de conformité]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[co-processed low-carbon-intensity fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[co-processed low-carbon-intensity
      fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the portion of a fuel that is
      produced from a mixture of a petroleum feedstock and a
      non-petroleum feedstock that are used simultaneously in the same
      processing unit of a petroleum refinery or upgrader facility and
      that is a low-carbon-intensity fuel derived from a non-petroleum
      feedstock.  ([combustible cotraité à faible intensité en
      carbone]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[co-processed low-carbon-intensity propane]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[co-processed low-carbon-intensity
      propane]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a co-processed
      low-carbon-intensity fuel that is a mixture that is gaseous at
      standard conditions and consists primarily of propane. ([propane
      cotraité à faible intensité en carbone]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[crop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[crop]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} includes a woody biomass crop with a
      rotational period that is not more than 25
      years. ([culture]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[deferred portion of the reduction requirements]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[deferred portion of the reduction
      requirements]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}, with respect to a compliance
      period, means the portion of the sum of the reduction requirements
      in respect of gasoline and diesel for that compliance period that
      has been deferred by a primary supplier in accordance with section
      16, as increased in accordance with section 17 and as reduced in
      accordance with section 18. ([partie reportée des exigences de
      réduction]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[diesel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[diesel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means liquid petroleum fuel that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is sold or represented as diesel or as a fuel
        suitable for use in a diesel engine; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} evaporates at atmospheric pressure, has a
        boiling point between 130°C and 400°C and is suitable for use in
        a diesel engine. ([diesel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[diesel replacement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[diesel replacement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a liquid
      low-carbon-intensity fuel that is suitable for use in a diesel
      engine, furnace or open flame burner or that is used in
      aviation. ([substitut du diesel]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[electric vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[electric vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle that is
      propelled by an electric motor whose source of electricity is a
      rechargeable battery that is charged from a source of electricity
      that is not on board the vehicle. It includes a plug-in hybrid
      electric vehicle. ([véhicule électrique]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[eligible feedstock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[eligible feedstock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a feedstock that
      is eligible under section 46 and meets the requirements set out in
      sections 48 to 52, except if it is exempted under any of sections
      53 to 55, as well as the requirements set out in section 57.
       ([charge d'alimentation admissible]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[EPA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[EPA]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the United States Environmental
      Protection Agency.  ([EPA]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[foreign supplier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[foreign supplier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the owner of a
      facility outside Canada at which a low-carbon-intensity fuel is
      produced or the person who leases, operates, controls or manages
      such a facility. ([fournisseur étranger]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Fuel LCA Model]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Fuel LCA Model]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the fuel life-cycle
      assessment model that is developed by the Minister in accordance
      with ISO Standard 14040 and that consists of the procedures that
      must be followed to determine the carbon intensity of a fuel,
      energy source or material input using life-cycle inventories for
      various pathways. ([modèle ACV des combustibles]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[fuelling station]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[fuelling station]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a facility in
      Canada at which vehicles are supplied with fuel or with hydrogen
      used as an energy source and includes a mobile facility. ([station
      de ravitaillement]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gaseous class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gaseous class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a class of fuel
      consisting of propane and natural gas. ([catégorie des
      combustibles gazeux]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gasoline]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means liquid petroleum fuel that

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} is sold or represented as gasoline, as a fuel
        suitable for use in a spark-ignition engine or as a fuel
        requiring only the addition of a low-carbon-intensity fuel or an
        oxygenate to make it suitable for use in a spark-ignition
        engine; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} is suitable for use in a spark-ignition engine
        and has, as determined by the applicable test method listed in
        National Standard of Canada CAN/CGSB-3.5-2021, entitled
        Automotive Gasoline, the following characteristics:

        - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a vapour pressure of no less than 38 kPa,

        - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an anti-knock index of no less than 80,

        - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a distillation temperature at which 10% of
          the fuel has evaporated of no less than 35°C and no more than
          70°C, and

        - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a distillation temperature at which 50% of
          the fuel has evaporated of no less than 65°C and no more than
          120°C. ([essence]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[gasoline replacement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[gasoline replacement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a liquid
      low-carbon-intensity fuel that is suitable for use in a
      spark-ignition engine.  ([substitut de l'essence]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[GPS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[GPS]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means global positioning
      system. ([GPS]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[hydrogen fuel cell vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[hydrogen fuel cell vehicle]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a vehicle
      propelled solely by an electric motor that uses electricity
      produced by an electrochemical cell from hydrogen. ([véhicule à
      pile à hydrogène]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ISO/IEC Standard 17011]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ISO/IEC Standard 17011]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means International
      Standard ISO/IEC 17011, entitled Conformity assessment ---
      Requirements for accreditation bodies accrediting conformity
      assessment bodies, published by the International Organization for
      Standardization. ([norme ISO/IEC 17011]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ISO/IEC Standard 17021-1]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ISO/IEC Standard 17021-1]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      International Standard ISO/IEC 17021-1, entitled Conformity
      assessment --- Requirements for bodies providing audit and
      certification of management systems --- Part 1: Requirements,
      published by the International Organization for
      Standardization ([norme ISO/IEC 17021-1]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[ISO/IEC Standard 17065]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ISO/IEC Standard 17065]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      International Standard ISO/IEC 17065, entitled Conformity
      assessment --- Requirements for bodies certifying products,
      processes and services, published by the International
      Organization for Standardization. ([norme ISO/IEC
      17065]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ISO Standard 14040]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ISO Standard 14040]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International
      Standard ISO 14040, entitled Environmental management --- Life
      cycle assessment --- Principles and framework, published by the
      International Organization for Standardization. ([norme ISO
      14040]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ISO Standard 14044]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ISO Standard 14044]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International
      Standard ISO 14044, entitled Environmental management --- Life
      cycle assessment --- Requirements and guidelines, published by the
      International Organization for Standardization. ([norme ISO
      14044]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ISO Standard 14064-2]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ISO Standard 14064-2]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      International Standard ISO 14064-2, entitled Greenhouse gases ---
      Part 2: Specification with guidance at the project level for
      quantification, monitoring and reporting of greenhouse gas
      emission reductions or removal enhancements, published by the
      International Organization for Standardization. ([norme ISO
      14064-2]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ISO Standard 14064-3:2019]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ISO Standard 14064-3:2019]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the
      International Standard ISO 14064-3:2019, entitled Greenhouse gases
      --- Part 3: Specification with guidance for the verification and
      validation of greenhouse gas statements, published by the
      International Organization for Standardization, as it read on May
      1, 2019. ([norme ISO 14064-3:2019]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[ISO Standard 19011]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[ISO Standard 19011]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the International
      Standard ISO 19011, entitled Guidelines for auditing management
      systems, published by the International Organization for
      Standardization. ([norme ISO 19011]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[liquid class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[liquid class]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a class of fuel
      consisting of the fossil fuels that are liquid at standard
      conditions.  ([catégorie des combustibles
      liquides]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[low-carbon-intensity fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[low-carbon-intensity fuel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a liquid
      or gaseous fuel that is not a fuel in the liquid class or gaseous
      class and that has a carbon intensity, for the compliance period
      during which the fuel was produced or imported, that does not
      exceed

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} 90% of the reference carbon intensity set out
        for that compliance period in item 1, column 2, of Schedule 1,
        in the case of a fuel that is in the liquid state at standard
        conditions;

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the reference carbon intensity set out for that
        compliance period in item 2, column 2, of Schedule 1, in the
        case of compressed renewable natural gas and liquefied renewable
        natural gas referred to in subsection 99(1), renewable natural
        gas referred to in subsection 100(1) or hydrogen referred to in
        paragraph 104(1)(b);

      - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the reference carbon intensity set out for that
        compliance period in item 3, column 2, of Schedule 1, in the
        case of renewable propane referred to in subsection 99(1) or
        100(1) and co-processed low-carbon-intensity propane referred to
        in subsection 99(1);

      - [(d)]{.lawlabel} 90% of the reference carbon intensity set out
        for that compliance period in item 2, column 2, of Schedule 1,
        in the case of biogas or in the case of renewable natural gas
        and hydrogen that is not referred to in paragraph (b); or

      - [(e)]{.lawlabel} 90% of the reference carbon intensity set out
        for that compliance period in item 3, column 2, of Schedule 1,
        in the case of renewable propane and co-processed
        low-carbon-intensity propane that is not referred to in
        paragraph (c). ([combustible à faible intensité en
        carbone]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[marine vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[marine vessel]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means any boat, ship or
      other vessel that is designed, used or capable of being used for
      navigation in, on or through water but is not designed for
      self-propulsion out of water. ([navire]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[Methods for Verification and Certification]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Methods for Verification and
      Certification]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the document entitled
      Methods for Verification and Certification --- Clean Fuel
      Regulations that is developed and published by the
      Minister. ([Méthodes de vérification et de
      certification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[misstatement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[misstatement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means an error, ommission or
      misreporting, as defined in Methods for Verification and
      Certification, in an application or report referred to in these
      Regulations.  ([déclaration erronée]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[participant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[participant]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a primary supplier who
      is registered with the Minister in accordance with subsection
      10(1) or a registered creator who participates in the
      compliance-credit transfer system. ([participant]{.DefinedTermLink
      lang="fr"})

  [[petroleum feedstock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[petroleum feedstock]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means crude oil or a
      substance derived from crude oil or natural gas, if it is
      primarily used as a feedstock to produce a fossil fuel in a
      petroleum refinery or upgrader facility, but it does not include
      any feedstock that is derived from petrochemicals or other
      hydrocarbon streams that have undergone additional processing,
      such as gas-to-liquid processing. ([charge d'alimentation à base
      de pétrole]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[primary supplier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[primary supplier]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person who

      - [(a)]{.lawlabel} owns, leases, operates, controls or manages a
        fuel production facility in Canada at which gasoline or diesel
        is produced; or

      - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imports gasoline or diesel into Canada.
        ([fournisseur principal]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[provisional compliance credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[provisional compliance credit]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a
      compliance credit referred to in subsection 23(1). ([unité de
      conformité provisoire]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[reduction requirement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[reduction requirement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the reduction
      requirement determined in accordance with section 9. ([exigence de
      réduction]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[registered creator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[registered creator]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a person
      registered with the Minister in accordance with subsection
      25(1). ([créateur enregistré]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[renewable natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[renewable natural gas]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means gas that meets
      the standard for injection into the closest natural gas pipeline
      and that is either synthetic natural gas derived from biomass or
      gas derived from the processing of biogas. ([gaz naturel
      renouvelable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[renewable propane]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[renewable propane]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a mixture that is
      gaseous at standard conditions, is recovered from the processing
      of biomass and consists primarily of propane, but it does not
      include co-processed low-carbon-intensity propane. ([propane
      renouvelable]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[residue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[residue]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a substance that is produced
      in a production process but whose production is not a primary aim
      of the process. It does not include any substance that the process
      has been deliberately modified to
      produce. ([résidu]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[scheme owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[scheme owner]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} has the same meaning as in
      subclause 3.11 of ISO/IEC Standard 17065. ([propriétaire du
      régime]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[Specifications for Fuel LCA Model CI Calculations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[Specifications for Fuel LCA Model CI
      Calculations]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the specifications that
      are developed and published by the Minister in respect of the
      calculation of the carbon intensity of a fuel, energy source or
      material input using the Fuel LCA Model. ([spécifications pour le
      calcul de l'IC au moyen du modèle ACV des
      combustibles]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[standard conditions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[standard conditions]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a temperature of
      15°C (59°F) and a pressure of 101.325 kPa (14.696
      psia). ([conditions normales]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[total reduction requirement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[total reduction requirement]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means the sum
      of the reduction requirements in respect of gasoline and diesel
      for the compliance period that ended most recently, and the
      deferred portion of the reduction requirements for each preceding
      compliance period. ([exigence de réduction
      totale]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

  [[verification body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm}

  :   [[verification body]{.dfn}]{.DefinedTerm} means a verification
      body referred to in section 137.  ([organisme de
      vérification]{.DefinedTermLink lang="fr"})

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Hydrogen used as energy source

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of these Regulations, hydrogen
  referred to in paragraph 104(1)(a) is deemed to be a
  low-carbon-intensity fuel if the carbon intensity of the hydrogen, for
  the compliance period during which the hydrogen is used, does not
  exceed the reference carbon intensity set out for that compliance
  period in item 2, column 2, of Schedule 1.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compressed gas and liquefied gas

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In these Regulations,

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} a reference to hydrogen includes compressed
    hydrogen and liquefied hydrogen;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} a reference to natural gas includes compressed
    natural gas and liquefied natural gas; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} a reference to renewable natural gas includes
    compressed renewable natural gas and liquefied renewable natural
    gas.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Co-processed low-carbon-intensity fuel

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The following provisions do not apply in respect of a
  co-processed low-carbon-intensity fuel:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraphs 19(1)(b) and (c);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraphs 20(b) and (c);

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} paragraphs 57(1)(b) and (2)(d);

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} subsection 59(2);

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} section 75;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} section 76;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} subsection 86(2) and (8);

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} section 91;

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} section 94;

  - [(j)]{.lawlabel} section 95;

  - [(k)]{.lawlabel} subsection 97(2);

  - [(l)]{.lawlabel} section 100;

  - [(m)]{.lawlabel} section 108;

  - [(n)]{.lawlabel} section 123;

  - [(o)]{.lawlabel} sections 5 and 6 of Schedule 3; and

  - [(p)]{.lawlabel} sections 1 to 6 of Schedule 14; and

  - [(q)]{.lawlabel} section 8 of Schedule 15.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Incorporation by reference

- **[[2]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-2 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  standard or method that is incorporated by reference in these
  Regulations, other than ISO Standard 14064-3:2019, is incorporated as
  amended from time to time.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Interpretation of documents
  incorporated by reference

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purpose of interpreting any document that is
  incorporated by reference in these Regulations, a reference to
  "should" in the English version of the document is to be read as
  "must" and any recommendation or suggestion is to be read as an
  obligation unless the context requires otherwise. For greater
  certainty, in the case of the accuracy or precision of a measurement,
  the context never requires otherwise.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Inconsistencies with these Regulations

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} In the event of an inconsistency between a provision
  in a document incorporated by reference into these Regulations and any
  provision of these Regulations, the provision of these Regulations
  prevails to the extent of the inconsistency.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Standard conditions

**[[3]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-3 .sectionLabel}** Unless otherwise specified,
a reference in these Regulations to a volume or quantity of gas or
liquid that is expressed in cubic metres is a reference to the volume or
quantity of that gas or liquid at standard conditions.

## [Application]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1358838 .Part}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exemption --- primary suppliers

- **[[4]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-4 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  primary supplier who produces in Canada or imports into Canada a
  volume of less than 400 m^3^ of gasoline or a volume of less than 400
  m^3^ of diesel during a compliance period is exempt from the
  application of these Regulations with respect to that fuel for that
  compliance period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- certain fuels

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} These Regulations do not apply in respect of gasoline
  or diesel that is

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} aviation gasoline;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} exported from Canada;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} used for scientific research, other than research
    on consumer preferences in respect of various properties of fuels or
    market research; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} sold or delivered for the purpose of supplying the
    engine of a vehicle, including a marine vessel, that is used
    exclusively for competition.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} However, a primary supplier who, during a compliance
  period, produces in Canada or imports into Canada a volume of 400 m^3^
  or more of gasoline or diesel must record the volume of every fuel
  referred to in paragraphs (2)(a) to (d) that is produced or imported
  during that compliance period and must include that information in the
  compliance report submitted to the Minister under section 127.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, the fuels referred to in
  paragraphs (2)(a) to (d) are not included when determining, for the
  purposes of subsection (1), the volume of fuel that is produced in
  Canada or imported into Canada by a primary supplier.

## [Requirements for Liquid Fuels]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1358852 .Part}

### [Carbon-Intensity Limits]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1358853 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Requirement --- carbon intensity

- **[[5]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-5 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of section 139 of the Act, a primary supplier's pool --- as
  determined in accordance with section 8 --- of a liquid fossil fuel
  that is set out in column 1 of the table to this subsection must not
  have a carbon intensity that is greater than the corresponding limit
  set out in column 2 for the corresponding compliance period.

  <figure id="1358857">
  <table
  class="table table-hover table-striped tablePointsize8 topdouble topbot">

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Lowering of carbon intensity

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A primary supplier must comply with subsection (1)
  with respect to a fuel for a compliance period by lowering the carbon
  intensity of their pool of that fuel for that compliance period by an
  amount that is equal to the difference between the baseline carbon
  intensity for that fuel and the limit set out in the table to
  subsection (1) for that fuel and that compliance period. The carbon
  intensity must be lowered by using compliance credits in accordance
  with section 11 to satisfy the reduction requirement for that
  compliance period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Baseline carbon intensity

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (2), the baseline
  carbon intensity of gasoline is 95 gCO~2~e/MJ and the baseline carbon
  intensity of diesel is 93 gCO~2~e/MJ.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of a fuel
  that is produced in Canada or imported into Canada before July 1,
  2023.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Volumetric requirement --- gasoline

- **[[6]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-6 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of section 139 of the Act, at least 5% of the volume of a
  primary supplier's pool of gasoline --- as determined in accordance
  with section 8 --- must, for each compliance period, be displaced by
  an equivalent volume of a gasoline replacement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- Newfoundland and Labrador

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the primary
  supplier may, for a compliance period, subtract from their pool of
  gasoline determined in accordance with section 8 any volume of
  gasoline that, during the compliance period, the primary supplier
  produced in or imported into Newfoundland and Labrador and sold or
  delivered for use in that province, if the primary supplier records
  information that establishes that the volume of gasoline met those
  conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of gasoline
  that is produced in Canada or imported into Canada before July 1,
  2023.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Volumetric requirement --- diesel

- **[[7]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-7 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For the
  purposes of section 139 of the Act, at least 2% of the volume of a
  primary supplier's pool of diesel --- as determined in accordance with
  section 8 --- must, for each compliance period, be displaced by an
  equivalent volume of a diesel replacement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- Newfoundland and Labrador

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection (1), the primary
  supplier may, for a compliance period, subtract from their pool of
  diesel determined in accordance with section 8 any volume of diesel
  that, during the compliance period, the primary supplier produced in
  or imported into Newfoundland and Labrador and sold or delivered for
  use in that province, if the primary supplier records information that
  establishes that the volume of diesel met those conditions.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subsection (1) does not apply in respect of diesel
  that is produced in Canada or imported into Canada before July 1,
  2023.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Pool of liquid fuel --- volume

- **[[8]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-8 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  primary supplier must, for each compliance period, determine the total
  volume, expressed in cubic metres, of their pool of gasoline or
  diesel, as the case may be, that was

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} produced by the primary supplier at a fuel
    production facility in Canada and, during the compliance period,
    either

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} dispatched from the fuel production facility, or

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} used to fuel a vehicle or other mobile equipment
      at the fuel production facility; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} imported by the primary supplier into Canada during
    the compliance period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Subtraction from pool

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} However, a primary supplier may, for each compliance
  period, subtract from their pool of gasoline or diesel, as the case
  may be, any volume of that fuel, if the primary supplier, before the
  August 1 of the calendar year that follows the end of the compliance
  period, records information that establishes that the volume of fuel
  met any of the following conditions during the compliance period:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} it was sold or delivered for a use other than
    combustion;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} it was sold or delivered for use in a marine vessel
    that had a non-Canadian port as its destination;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} it was sold or delivered for use for non-industrial
    purposes in a geographic area that is served by neither an
    electrical distribution network that is subject to the standards of
    the North American Electric Reliability Corporation nor a natural
    gas distribution system;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} it was sold or delivered for the purpose of space
    heating;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} it was sold or delivered for use in the generation
    of electricity in a geographic area that is served by neither an
    electrical distribution network that is subject to the standards of
    the North American Electric Reliability Corporation nor a natural
    gas distribution system.

### [Reduction Requirement]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1358898 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduction in tonnes

**[[9]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-9 .sectionLabel}** The carbon intensity of a
pool of gasoline or diesel for a compliance period is considered to be
lowered for the purposes of subsection 5(2) if the number of tonnes of
CO~2~e released over the life cycle of that fuel is reduced by the value
of the reduction requirement for that compliance period, as determined
by the formula

::: {#1358901 .Section}
CI~diff~ × (Q × D) × 10^-6^

where

[CI~diff~]{.dfn}
:   is the difference, expressed in gCO~2~e/MJ, between the baseline
    carbon intensity of that fuel, as set out in subsection 5(3), and
    the limit for that fuel for that compliance period, as set out in
    column 2 of the table to subsection 5(1);

[Q]{.dfn}
:   is the volume of that pool for the compliance period, as determined
    in accordance with section 8 and expressed in cubic metres; and

[D]{.dfn}
:   is, at the election of the primary supplier, the energy density of
    the fuel as set out in column 2 of Schedule 2 or as set out in the
    Specifications for Fuel LCA Model CI Calculations.
:::

### [Registration as Primary Supplier]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1358906 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration report

- **[[10]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-10 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  primary supplier must register with the Minister by submitting a
  registration report to the Minister that contains the information
  referred to in sections 1 to 3 of Schedule 3 no later than 45 days
  after the day on which they have produced in Canada or imported into
  Canada, during a compliance period, a total volume of 400 m^3^ or more
  of gasoline or a total volume of 400 m^3^ or more of diesel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- registration within 90
  days

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} However, the registration report may be submitted at
  any time during the period that begins on the day on which these
  Regulations are registered and ends 90 days after that day.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of information

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} If there are any changes in the information referred
  to in section 1 of Schedule 3 that is provided in the registration
  report, the primary supplier must send a notice to the Minister that
  provides the updated information within 30 days after the day on which
  the change occurs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Notice of cancellation

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A primary supplier who is not required by
  subsection (1) to be registered for a given compliance period and who
  has complied with the requirements set out in these Regulations for
  all of the previous compliance periods, including the volumetric
  requirements set out in subsections 6(1) and 7(1) and the reduction
  requirement, may cancel their registration as a primary supplier by
  sending a notice to that effect to the Minister.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation by Minister

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} If, after receiving the notice referred to in
  subsection (4), the Minister is satisfied that the primary supplier
  has complied with these Regulations for all previous compliance
  periods, the Minister must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} if the primary supplier is not a registered
    creator, cancel any compliance credits in their accounts opened
    under section 28;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} cancel their registration as a primary supplier;
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} send a notice to the primary supplier informing
    them that their registration has been cancelled.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Clarification

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} For greater certainty, subsection (1) applies to a
  primary supplier whose registration was cancelled by the Minister
  under subsection (5).

## [Compliance Credits]{.HTitleText1} {#h-1358923 .Part}

### [Use]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1358924 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Satisfaction of reduction requirement

- **[[11]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-11 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  primary supplier must use the compliance credits that they create
  under sections 19 and 20, or that are transferred to them under the
  compliance-credit transfer system, to satisfy the total reduction
  requirement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed reduction

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The use of one compliance credit by a primary
  supplier for gasoline or diesel that is produced in Canada or imported
  into Canada during a compliance period is deemed to reduce by one
  tonne the quantity of CO~2~e released over the life cycle of that fuel
  during that compliance period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prior creation of provisional credit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The primary supplier must use only the compliance
  credits that are created as provisional compliance credits before the
  end of a compliance period or the compliance credits that they create
  under subsection 19(2) to satisfy the reduction requirement for that
  compliance period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation after use

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must cancel a compliance credit
  immediately after it is used.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed compliance --- gasoline

- **[[12]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-12 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 6(1), a compliance credit that was created
  under paragraph 19(1)(a), (b) or (c) by producing in Canada or
  importing into Canada a volume of a gasoline replacement, and that is
  used by a primary supplier in accordance with section 11 for a
  compliance period, is deemed to displace, for that compliance period,
  the use of an equivalent volume of the primary supplier's pool of
  gasoline.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deemed compliance --- diesel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 7(1), a compliance
  credit that was created under paragraph 19(1)(a), (b) or (c) by
  producing in Canada or importing into Canada a volume of a diesel
  replacement, and that was used by a primary supplier in accordance
  with section 11 for a compliance period, is deemed to displace, for
  that compliance period, the use of an equivalent volume of the primary
  supplier's pool of diesel.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Prior creation of provisional credit

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The primary supplier must use only the compliance
  credits that are created as provisional compliance credits before the
  end of a compliance period to comply with the volumetric requirements
  set out in subsections 6(1) and 7(1) for that compliance period.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}July 31 --- gasoline

- **[[13]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-13 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 12(1), a primary supplier must, no later
  than the July 31 that follows the end of a compliance period, use ---
  in accordance with subsection 12(3) --- the compliance credits that
  are in their account opened under paragraph 28(a), until

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the volumetric requirement set out in subsection
    6(1) is met for that compliance period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no such compliance credits remain in that account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}July 31 --- diesel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 12(2), a primary
  supplier must, no later than the July 31 that follows the end of a
  compliance period, use --- in accordance with subsection 12(3) --- the
  compliance credits that are in their account opened under paragraph
  28(a), until

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the volumetric requirement set out in subsection
    7(1) is met for that compliance period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no such compliance credits remain in that account.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}July 31 --- contribution to funding
  program

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 11 and subject to
  subsection 15(1), a primary supplier must, no later than the July 31
  that follows the end of a compliance period, use all of the compliance
  credits that they created by contributing to a registered
  emission-reduction funding program in accordance with paragraph
  118(1)(a) to satisfy the reduction requirement for that compliance
  period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}July 31 --- reduction requirement

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 11 and subject to
  subsections 15(2) and (3), a primary supplier must, no later than the
  July 31 that follows the end of a compliance period, use the
  compliance credits that are in their accounts opened under section 28,
  until

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the reduction requirement is satisfied for that
    compliance period; or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} no such compliance credits remain in those
    accounts.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Choice of compliance credits

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsections (1) and (2), if the
  circumstances described in paragraph (4)(a) occur before the
  circumstances described in paragraph (4)(b), the primary supplier must
  satisfy the reduction requirement by using compliance credits that are
  chosen by them and indicated in the report submitted under subsection
  127(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}August 1 --- cancellation of credits

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} On the August 1 that follows the end of a compliance
  period, if any of the compliance credits referred to in subsection (3)
  have not been used by the primary supplier, the Minister must cancel
  those credits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- subsections (1) to
  (4)

  [(7)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (4) do not apply in respect of any
  compliance period that ends before July 1, 2023.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}December 15 --- gasoline

- **[[14]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-14 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} For
  the purposes of subsection 12(1), if a primary supplier, on the August
  1 that follows the end of a compliance period, has not complied with
  the volumetric requirement set out in subsection 6(1) for that
  compliance period, the primary supplier must, no later than the
  following December 15, comply with that requirement by using the
  compliance credits transferred to them under section 112.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}December 15 --- diesel

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of subsection 12(2), if a primary
  supplier, on the August 1 that follows the end of a compliance period,
  has not complied with the volumetric requirement set out in subsection
  7(1) for that compliance period, the primary supplier must, no later
  than the following December 15, comply with that requirement by using
  the compliance credits transferred to them under section 112.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Other credits

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} For the purposes of section 11 and subject to
  subsection 15(1), if a primary supplier, on the August 1 that follows
  the end of a compliance period, has not satisfied the reduction
  requirement for that compliance period, the primary supplier must, no
  later than the December 15 that follows the end of that compliance
  period, use the compliance credits that were transferred to them under
  section 112, or that they created by contributing to a registered
  emission-reduction funding program in accordance with paragraph
  118(1)(b), to satisfy that reduction requirement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance --- December 15

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} Subject to sections 16 to 18, a primary supplier
  must, no later than the December 15 that follows the end of a
  compliance period, use the compliance credits in their accounts opened
  under section 28 to satisfy the total reduction requirement and comply
  with the volumetric requirements set out in subsections 6(1) and 7(1)
  for that compliance period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}December 16 --- cancellation

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} On the December 16 that follows the expiry of a
  compliance period, if any of the compliance credits that are referred
  to in subsection (3) have not been used by the primary supplier, the
  Minister must cancel those credits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application --- subsections (1) to
  (4)

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (4) do not apply in respect of any
  compliance period that ends before July 1, 2023.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limits on credit use --- funding program

- **[[15]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-15 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  primary supplier must ensure that the total number of compliance
  credits created under subsection 19(2) --- that are used in accordance
  with subsections 13(3) and 14(3), paragraph 18(1)(a) and subsection
  18(3) during the calendar year that follows the end of a compliance
  period --- does not exceed 10% of the primary supplier's total
  reduction requirement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit --- paragraph 28(b)

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The primary supplier must ensure that the total
  number of compliance credits in their account opened under paragraph
  28(b) --- that are used in accordance with subsection 13(4) and
  paragraph 18(1)(b) during the calendar year that follows the end of a
  compliance period --- does not exceed 10% of the primary supplier's
  total reduction requirement.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Limit --- generic quantification method

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The primary supplier must ensure that the total
  number of compliance credits created under paragraph 19(1)(a) in
  respect of a project for which a generic emission-reduction
  quantification method is applicable --- that are used in accordance
  with subsection 13(4) and paragraphs 18(1)(b) and (c) during the
  calendar year that follows the end of a compliance period --- does not
  exceed 10% of the primary supplier's total reduction requirement.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deferral of reduction requirements

- **[[16]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-16 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  primary supplier may, no later than the December 15 that follows the
  end of a compliance period, defer satisfaction of their reduction
  requirements for that compliance period by any number of compliance
  credits that does not exceed the greater of zero and the result of the
  formula

  ::: {#1358985 .Subsection}
  10% x R~current~ − R~deferred~

  where

  [R~current~]{.dfn}
  :   is the sum of their reduction requirements in respect of gasoline
      and diesel for that compliance period; and

  [R~deferred~]{.dfn}
  :   is the sum of all deferred portions of the reduction requirements
      in respect of gasoline and diesel for previous compliance periods.
  :::

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions for deferral

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The primary supplier may defer a portion of their
  reduction requirements for a compliance period under subsection (1)
  only if

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the Minister has sent a notice in accordance with
    subsection 111(2) for that compliance period or the primary supplier
    has acquired by transfer through the compliance-credit clearance
    mechanism the number of compliance credits determined in accordance
    with paragraph 112(5)(b) for that compliance period; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the primary supplier has created enough compliance
    credits by contributing to a registered emission-reduction funding
    program to satisfy 10% of the total reduction requirement for that
    compliance period in respect of all types of fuels.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Compliance required after five years

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A primary supplier who defers under subsection (1) a
  portion of their reduction requirements for a compliance period must
  satisfy the deferred portion no later than the December 15 that
  follows the fifth anniversary of the end of that compliance period.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Increase to deferred portion

**[[17]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-17 .sectionLabel}** On each December 16 that
follows the end of a compliance period for which a portion of the
reduction requirements is deferred under subsection 16(1) and that
precedes the fifth anniversary of the end of that compliance period, the
deferred portion of the reduction requirements is to be multiplied by
1.05.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduction --- July 31

- **[[18]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-18 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} In
  order to reduce a deferred portion of the reduction requirements in
  accordance with section 11, the primary supplier must, during the
  period that begins on the day after a compliance period expires and
  ends on the July 31 that follows that day, use the following
  compliance credits that are in their accounts opened under section 28
  and that exceed the number required to satisfy the reduction
  requirements for the compliance period that ended most recently:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 15(1), all of the compliance
    credits that they created by contributing to a registered
    emission-reduction funding program in accordance with paragraph
    118(1)(a);

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsections 15(2) and (3), any
    compliance credits in their account opened under paragraph 28(b);
    and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} subject to subsection 15(3), any compliance credits
    in their account opened under paragraph 28(a).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Election

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the primary supplier has more credits than the
  number required to satisfy the deferred portion of the reduction
  requirements in accordance with subsection (1), they may elect to use
  any number of compliance credits referred to in paragraphs (1)(b)
  and (c) to satisfy the deferred portion of the reduction requirements.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Reduction --- December 15

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} Subject to subsection 15(1), if a primary supplier,
  on the August 1 that follows the end of a compliance period, has not
  satisfied the deferred portion of the reduction requirements for a
  previous compliance period in accordance with subsection (2), the
  primary supplier must, no later than the following December 15, reduce
  the deferred portion of the reduction requirements by using all of the
  compliance credits in their account opened under paragraph 28(a) ---
  that were transferred to them through the compliance-credit clearance
  mechanism under section 112 or that they created by contributing to a
  registered emission-reduction funding program in accordance with
  paragraph 118(1)(b) --- that exceed the number of compliance credits
  required to satisfy their reduction requirements for the compliance
  period that ended most recently.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Multiple compliance periods

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} If a primary supplier has not satisfied the deferred
  portion of the reduction requirements for more than one previous
  compliance period, they must not use any compliance credits to reduce
  the deferred portion of the reduction requirements in accordance with
  subsection (1) or (3) for a particular compliance period unless they
  have satisfied the deferred portion of the reduction requirements for
  every compliance period that precedes that particular compliance
  period.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Non-application

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} Subsections (1) to (4) do not apply before January 1,
  2025.

### [Creation]{.HTitleText2} {#h-1359011 .Subheading}

#### [Reduction of CO~2~e Emissions]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1359012 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Liquid class

- **[[19]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-19 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  registered creator may create a compliance credit in respect of the
  liquid class in the following cases:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} the registered creator, or a person with whom they
    have entered into an agreement under section 21, lowers the carbon
    intensity of a fuel in the liquid class by carrying out, in respect
    of that fuel, a CO~2~e-emission-reduction project that is described
    in section 30 and that is recognized under subsection 35(1), 36(1),
    37(3), 39(1) or 40(3), as the case may be;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} the registered creator imports into Canada a
    quantity of low-carbon-intensity fuel to which may be attributed a
    reduction in the number of tonnes of CO~2~e that would otherwise
    have been emitted by the use of a fuel in the liquid class, if the
    low-carbon-intensity fuel

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is in the liquid state at standard conditions,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is one referred to in section 94,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is used or sold for use as a fuel in Canada
      whether as neat fuel or as part of a blend, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements set out in section 56;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} the registered creator, or a person with whom they
    have entered into an agreement under section 21, produces in Canada
    a quantity of low-carbon-intensity fuel to which may be attributed a
    reduction in the number of tonnes of CO~2~e that would otherwise
    have been emitted by the use of a fuel in the liquid class, if the
    low-carbon-intensity fuel

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is in the liquid state at standard conditions,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is one referred to in section 94,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is used or sold for use as a fuel in Canada
      whether as neat fuel or as part of a blend, and

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements set out in section 56; or

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} the registered creator, or a person with whom they
    have entered into an agreement under section 21, reduces the number
    of tonnes of CO~2~e emissions by displacing the use in Canada of a
    quantity of fuel in the liquid class as a fuel for a vehicle with
    the use in Canada of

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} a quantity of propane, compressed natural gas or
      liquefied natural gas as a fuel for a vehicle, in accordance with
      section 98,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} a quantity of renewable propane, co-processed
      low-carbon-intensity propane, compressed renewable natural gas or
      liquefied renewable natural gas that meets the requirements set
      out in section 56 as a fuel for a vehicle, in accordance with
      section 99,

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} a quantity of renewable propane or renewable
      natural gas that meets the requirements set out in section 56 as a
      fuel for a vehicle, in accordance with section 100,

    - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} a quantity of electricity as an energy source
      for an electric vehicle, in accordance with sections 101 and 102,
      or

    - [(v)]{.lawlabel} a quantity of hydrogen that meets the
      requirements set out in section 56, in accordance with section
      104,

      - [(A)]{.lawlabel} as an energy source for a hydrogen fuel cell
        vehicle, or

      - [(B)]{.lawlabel} as a fuel for a vehicle other than a hydrogen
        fuel cell vehicle.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Contribution to funding program

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A primary supplier may create a compliance credit in
  respect of the liquid class if they make a contribution to a
  registered emission-reduction funding program in accordance with
  section 118.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Gaseous class

**[[20]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-20 .sectionLabel}** A registered creator may
create a compliance credit in respect of the gaseous class in the
following cases:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} the registered creator, or a person with whom they
  have entered into an agreement under section 21, lowers the carbon
  intensity of a fuel in the gaseous class by carrying out, in respect
  of that fuel, a CO~2~e-emission-reduction project described in
  paragraph 30(d) that reduces the carbon intensity of a fuel in the
  liquid class and is recognized under subsection 36(1), 37(3), 39(1) or
  40(3), as the case may be;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the registered creator imports into Canada a quantity
  of low-carbon-intensity fuel to which may be attributed a reduction in
  the number of tonnes of CO~2~e that would otherwise have been emitted
  by the use of a fuel in the gaseous class, if the low-carbon-intensity
  fuel

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is biogas, renewable natural gas, renewable propane
    or hydrogen,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is one referred to in section 95,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is used or sold for use as a fuel in Canada
    whether as neat fuel or as part of a blend, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements set out in section 56; or

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the registered creator, or a person with whom they
  have entered into an agreement under section 21, produces in Canada a
  quantity of low-carbon-intensity fuel to which may be attributed a
  reduction in the number of tonnes of CO~2~e that would otherwise have
  been emitted by the use of a fuel in the gaseous class, if the
  low-carbon-intensity fuel

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is biogas, renewable natural gas, renewable propane
    or hydrogen,

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} is one referred to in section 95 or 96, as the
    case may be,

  - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} is used or sold for use as a fuel in Canada
    whether as neat fuel or as part of a blend, and

  - [(iv)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements set out in section 56.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Agreement to create credits

- **[[21]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-21 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  registered creator may, before they have created any provisional
  compliance credits, enter into an agreement to create compliance
  credits for a compliance period

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with a person who is carrying out a
    CO~2~e-emission-reduction project described in section 30 or a
    person who is referred to in subsection 98(1), 99(1), 101(1), 102(1)
    or 104(1); or

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} with a producer who is referred to in subsection
    94(1), 95(1), 96(1) or 100(1), if the agreement applies only to the
    creation of compliance credits through the production of a quantity
    of low-carbon-intensity fuel in Canada.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Validity of agreement --- requirements

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The agreement is not valid unless it is signed by the
  authorized agents of each of the parties to the agreement and contains
  the following information:

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} with respect to each party to the agreement,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} their name, civic address, postal address,
      telephone number and, if any, email address,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic address, postal address,
      telephone number and, if any, email address of their authorized
      agent, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} the name, title, civic address, postal address,
      telephone number and, if any, email address of a contact person,
      unless the contact person is the authorized agent;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the compliance period to which the
    agreement relates;

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} if the agreement relates to a
    CO~2~e-emission-reduction project described in section 30,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name of the facility at which the project is
      carried out or, if the equipment with which it is carried out is
      not within a facility, the name of that equipment,

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the GPS coordinates to the fifth decimal place
      and, if any, civic address of that facility or equipment, and

    - [(iii)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the type of co-processed
      low-carbon-intensity fuel produced at the facility, if applicable;

  - [(d)]{.lawlabel} if the agreement relates to the production of the
    fuel referred to in subsection 94(1), 95(1), 96(1) or 100(1) in a
    facility, with respect to each facility where the fuel is produced,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name, GPS coordinates to the fifth decimal
      place and, if any, civic address of that facility, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the type of fuel produced at
      the facility;

  - [(e)]{.lawlabel} if the agreement relates to the supply of a fuel
    referred to in subsection 98(1) or 99(1) at fuelling stations, with
    respect to each fuelling station,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} the name, GPS coordinates to the fifth decimal
      place and, if any, civic address of the fuelling station, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the type of fuel supplied by
      the fuelling station;

  - [(f)]{.lawlabel} if the agreement is with a charging-network
    operator referred to in subsection 102(1), with respect to each
    charging station in the charging-network operator's network,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the province in which it is
      located, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} an indication of whether it is intended
      primarily for use by the occupants of a private dwelling-place,
      for use by the public or for another use;

  - [(g)]{.lawlabel} if the agreement is with a charging-site host
    referred to in subsection 101(1), with respect to each charging
    station that the charging-site host owns or leases,

    - [(i)]{.lawlabel} an indication of the province in which it is
      located, and

    - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} the name of the charging-network operator, if
      any, who operates the communication platform that collects data
      from the charging station; and

  - [(h)]{.lawlabel} if the agreement is with an owner or operator
    referred to in subsection 104(1) of one or more hydrogen fuelling
    stations, the name, GPS coordinates to the fifth decimal place and,
    if any, civic address of each fuelling station.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Submission to Minister

- **[[22]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-22 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  registered creator who enters into an agreement referred to in section
  21 must submit the agreement to the Minister and, subject to
  subsection (2), must not create provisional compliance credits under
  the agreement until the day after the day on which it is submitted.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- submission within first
  60 days

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If the agreement is submitted to the Minister during
  the first 60 days of the compliance period to which the agreement
  relates, the registered creator may create provisional compliance
  credits under the agreement as of the first day of that compliance
  period unless the agreement provides for a later date.

#### [Creation of Provisional Compliance Credits]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1359084 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Creation of provisional compliance
credits

- **[[23]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-23 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} Any
  compliance credit created under subsection 19(1) or section 20 is
  considered to be a provisional compliance credit at the time that it
  is created.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}No use of provisional credit

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A primary supplier must not use a provisional
  compliance credit to satisfy a total reduction requirement or to
  comply with any of the volumetric requirements set out in subsections
  6(1) and 7(1) and must not transfer a provisional compliance credit
  under the compliance-credit transfer system.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Single use for creation of provisional
  credits

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A quantity of a fuel or an energy source that has
  been used by a person to create provisional compliance credits under
  subsection 94(1), 95(1), 96(1), 98(1), 99(1), 100(1), 101(1), 102(1)
  or 104(1) must not be used by another person to create compliance
  credits under the same subsection, and any quantity that is used more
  than once under the same subsection is deemed not to create any
  provisional compliance credits.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Loss of provisional status

  [(4)]{.lawlabel} A provisional compliance credit that is the subject
  of a credit-creation report submitted under section 120 or 121 ceases
  to be provisional when the Minister deposits it into a
  compliance-credit account under subsection 24(1) or (2).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Ownership of provisional credits

  [(5)]{.lawlabel} On creation, a provisional compliance credit is owned
  by the registered creator who created it.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Single owner

  [(6)]{.lawlabel} At any given time, a provisional compliance credit
  may only have a single owner.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposit into account

- **[[24]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-24 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  compliance credits that are the subject of a credit-creation report
  submitted under section 120 or 121 must be deposited by the Minister
  as soon as feasible after the reception of the report into the
  registered creator's account that was opened under

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 28(a), in the case of a compliance credit
    created under subsection 19(1); and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} paragraph 28(b), in the case of a compliance credit
    created under section 20.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Deposit --- adjustment of credits

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The compliance credits created under section 88, 89
  or 90 that are the subject of a credit-creation report submitted under
  section 120 or a credit-adjustment report submitted under section 122
  must be deposited by the Minister as soon as feasible after the
  reception of the report into any of the registered creator's
  compliance-credit accounts opened under section 28.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Identification number

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} The Minister must assign an identification number to
  each compliance credit when it is deposited into a compliance-credit
  account.

#### [Registration as Registered Creator]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1359107 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration report

- **[[25]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-25 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  person who wishes to create compliance credits under subsection 19(1)
  or section 20 or under an agreement referred to in section 21 may
  register with the Minister as a registered creator by submitting to
  the Minister a registration report containing the information referred
  to in section 1 of Schedule 3 and any applicable information referred
  to in sections 4 to 12 of that Schedule.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Registration before creation

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} A registered creator must not create provisional
  compliance credits under subsection 19(1) or section 20 until the day
  after the day on which they become a registered creator.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Exception --- registration within 60
  days

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} However, a person who submits a registration report
  to the Minister during the period that begins on the day on which
  these Regulations are registered and ends 60 days after that day may
  create provisional compliance credits as of that day.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Change of information

- **[[26]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-26 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} If
  there are any changes in the information referred to in section 1 of
  Schedule 3 that is provided in the registration report, the registered
  creator must send a notice to the Minister that provides the updated
  information within 30 days after the day on which the change occurs.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Sections 4 to 12 of Schedule 3

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If there are any changes in the information referred
  to in sections 4 to 12 of Schedule 3 that is provided in the
  registration report, the registered creator must send a notice to the
  Minister that provides the updated information no later than on the
  next day on which they are required to submit a report under
  subsection 120(1) or 121(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}New agreement

  [(3)]{.lawlabel} A registered creator who enters into an agreement
  under section 21 must send a notice to the Minister that contains, in
  respect of the activities carried out by the person with whom they
  have entered into the agreement, the information referred to in
  section 1 of Schedule 3 and any applicable information referred to in
  sections 4 to 12 of that Schedule.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation of registration

- **[[27]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-27 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} A
  registered creator may cancel their registration as a registered
  creator if they

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} send to the Minister a notice that indicates their
    intention to cancel their registration; and

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} submit to the Minister any report or send any
    notice that they are required by these Regulations to submit or
    send.

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Cancellation by Minister

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} If, after receiving the notice referred to in
  paragraph (1)(a), the Minister is satisfied that the registered
  creator has complied with these Regulations, the Minister must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} in the case of a registered creator who is not a
    primary supplier, cancel any compliance credits in any of the
    registered creator's accounts opened under section 28;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} cancel the registered creator's registration as a
    registered creator; and

  - [(c)]{.lawlabel} send a notice to the registered creator informing
    them that their registration is cancelled.

#### [Compliance-Credit Accounts]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1359132 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Opening

**[[28]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-28 .sectionLabel}** On the registration of a
primary supplier under subsection 10(1) or a registered creator under
subsection 25(1), the Minister must open the following accounts for the
primary supplier or the registered creator in the compliance-credit
transfer system:

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a liquid-fuel-compliance-credit account; and

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} a gaseous-fuel-compliance-credit account.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Credit remains in account

**[[29]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-29 .sectionLabel}** A compliance credit that
is deposited into an account must remain in that account until the
compliance credit is cancelled or transferred.

#### [CO~2~e-Emission-Reduction Project]{.HTitleText3} {#h-1359139 .Subheading}

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Series of activities

**[[30]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-30 .sectionLabel}** A
CO~2~e-emission-reduction project must consist of a series of activities
that, when carried out, result in

- [(a)]{.lawlabel} a reduction of the number of tonnes of CO~2~e
  emissions that are released during the activities carried out over the
  life cycle of a fossil fuel in the liquid state at standard
  conditions, including all emissions associated with the extraction of
  the hydrocarbons used to produce the fuel, with the processing,
  refining or upgrading of those hydrocarbons to produce the fuel, with
  the transportation or distribution of those hydrocarbons or the fuel
  and with the combustion of the fuel;

- [(b)]{.lawlabel} the sequestration of any CO~2~e emissions that are
  released during the activities carried out over the life cycle of a
  fossil fuel in the liquid state at standard conditions, including all
  emissions associated with the extraction of the hydrocarbons used to
  produce the fuel, with the processing, refining or upgrading of those
  hydrocarbons to produce the fuel, with the transportation or
  distribution of those hydrocarbons or the fuel and with the combustion
  of the fuel;

- [(c)]{.lawlabel} the use of any CO~2~e emissions that are released
  during the activities carried out over the life cycle of a fossil fuel
  in the liquid state at standard conditions, including all emissions
  associated with the extraction of the hydrocarbons used to produce the
  fuel, with processing, refining or upgrading those hydrocarbons to
  produce the fuel, with the transportation or distribution of those
  hydrocarbons or the fuel and with the combustion of the fuel; or

- [(d)]{.lawlabel} the production of a quantity of co-processed
  low-carbon-intensity fuel to which may be attributed a reduction in
  the number of tonnes of CO~2~e that would otherwise have been emitted
  by the use of a fuel in the liquid class or gaseous class, if the
  co-processed low-carbon-intensity fuel

  - [(i)]{.lawlabel} is used or sold for use as a fuel in Canada whether
    as neat fuel or as part of a blend, and

  - [(ii)]{.lawlabel} meets the requirements set out in section 56.

[Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Generic quantification method

- **[[31]{.sectionLabel}]{#s-31 .sectionLabel}** [(1)]{.lawlabel} The
  Minister may establish a generic emission-reduction quantification
  method that is applicable to any project for which no specific
  emission-reduction quantification method is applicable on the day on
  which the project is recognized under subsection 35(1) or 39(1).

- [Marginal note:]{.wb-invisible}Conditions

  [(2)]{.lawlabel} The generic emission-reduction quantification method
  must

  - [(a)]{.lawlabel} be consistent with ISO Standard 14064-2;

  - [(b)]{.lawlabel} establish a period of 10 years, beginning on the
    later of the day on which the Minister recognizes a project to which
    it is applicable and any preferred day referred to in paragraph
    34(2)(b), at the end of which the carrying out of the project ceases
